    --  --
    ONEOF 'BTHB Core 0 Hole Size' | var=AmdSetup+0x1B5 w=1B range=0..4 step=0 qid=0x009E

      opt '4 MB' = 3
      opt '8 MB' = 4
    ONEOF 'BTHB Core 1 Hole Size' | var=AmdSetup+0x1B6 w=1B range=0..4 step=0 qid=0x009F
